# V1-V22 unified failure diagnostic

This report is generated from the latest metadata-only `final_results` snapshot. It keeps source batches separate. Exact paired Delta ARI requires the same dataset, seed, readout, input protocol, and budget; published aggregate pairs are retained only when the source artifact explicitly supplies the match, with their scope recorded in the long table.

- Source snapshot: `result/final_results`
- Snapshot SHA256 (recursive path+content): `97e399f41fbe42faa4bf46432bc149da517a1c22b376f975d4e86ce37ed8700e`
- Generated: `2026-08-14T01:26:56.159000+00:00`
- Long rows: `2209`; completed rows: `2175`; rows with paired Delta ARI: `1637`; paired regressions below -0.03: `680`

## Family semantics

`self` is the same-batch no-gate/no-mix reference where available. `fixed`, `random`, `static`, and `learned` retain source semantics; V19 no-mix/reliability ablations remain under `learned` because they still use the learned RG path, while V19 constant-gate is `static`. `hard` is the explicit hard-selection family (V13 topk2 and the V22 hard branch). `assignment-adversarial` is the V21 assignment-objective intervention. `discriminator` is the V22 discriminator-backed branch; the V22 hard branch is intentionally aliased into both `hard` and `discriminator` views. `NA` means the final snapshot did not publish a matched control or a usage denominator.

## Interpretation boundaries

- `NA - no paired control` is not a zero effect. It means the snapshot cannot support a paired claim.
- V20 is single-seed and has no matched control; its effective mask is not a paired performance result.
- V21 is the formal v2 Student-t-head graph-fix readout. A clean-embedding KMeans audit is a separate readout and is not merged here.
- V22 hard and cooperative branches are single-seed; the cooperative panel retains `incomplete_compute` rows and has no matched scMAE-only baseline.
- V18/V19 group summaries are matched aggregate means where raw per-seed rows are absent from the publication snapshot; their scope is explicitly recorded in `paired_delta_scope`.

## Reproduction

```bash
python scripts/analysis/build_v1_v22_failure_diagnostic.py --final-results-root <path-to-final_results>
```
